TRACTATUS DE SORTILEGIIS — Paolo Grillandi (Paulus Grillandus, c. 1490-?)
From: Tractatus duo (Frankfurt: Martin Lechler, 1591/1592) — first treatise of the volume.
The second treatise in the volume is Gianfrancesco Ponzinibio's De lamiis (staged separately).
